### Common Anti-patterns

Learning what NOT to do often provides more value than best practices. These patterns consistently cause problems:

### Cost Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Using Opus for everything | 5x cost, often unnecessary | Default Sonnet, Opus only for architecture |
| Never checking`/cost` | Surprise bills | Check cost after major tasks |
| Extended thinking on simple tasks | Wasted tokens | Toggle off (Alt+T) for routine work |
| Running explore in main context | Context bloat | Use Explore subagent instead |

### Context Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Ignoring context until it bloats | Degraded reasoning, forgotten decisions | `/compact` proactively at 50% capacity |
| Reading entire files when you need sections | Wastes context on irrelevant code | Reference specific line ranges |
| Never using subagents | Everything fills main context | Delegate exploration and analysis |
| Giant CLAUDE.md files | Context waste every session | Keep it under 500 lines, use imports |

### Workflow Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Creating overlapping skills and commands | Confusion, unpredictable behavior | One mechanism per purpose |
| Prompts for guaranteed actions | Claude may skip or forget | Use hooks for must-run actions |
| No hooks for formatting | Inconsistent code style | Hook every Edit/Write for formatter |
| Allowing all bash by default | Security risk | Explicit allowlist for safe commands |

### Configuration Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| All config in user settings | Nothing shared with team | Project settings for team standards |
| Committing personal preferences | Overrides teammates | Use`settings.local.json` for personal |
| No deny rules | Claude can touch sensitive files | Deny`.env`, credentials, secrets |
| Ignoring managed settings | Enterprise policies bypassed | Managed settings for compliance |

### Prompt Anti-patterns

| Anti-pattern | Problem | Solution |
| --- | --- | --- |
| “Make it better” | Vague, poor results | Specify exactly what “better” means |
| Not referencing files | Claude guesses paths | Use`@path/to/file.ts` syntax |
| Ignoring Claude’s questions | Works on wrong assumptions | Answer before proceeding |
| Providing full docs when snippet suffices | Context waste | Extract relevant sections |

## Workflow Recipes

End-to-end patterns for common scenarios.

### Recipe 1: Starting a New Project

```
# 1. Initialize
cd my-project
claude

# 2. Let Claude understand the codebase
> "Explore this codebase and summarize the architecture"

# 3. Set up project configuration
> /init

# 4. Create CLAUDE.md with learned patterns
> "Create CLAUDE.md documenting the patterns you observed"

# 5. Add team commands
> "Create a /test command that runs our test suite"
> "Create a /deploy command for staging deployment"

```

### Recipe 2: Daily Development Flow

```
# 1. Continue yesterday's session
claude -c

# 2. Quick context recap
> "What did we work on yesterday?"

# 3. Check current state
> /status

# 4. Work on feature (Sonnet handles most tasks)
> "Implement the user profile endpoint"

# 5. Proactive context management
> /compact # Before context exceeds 50%

# 6. Check spending
> /cost

# 7. End session cleanly
> "Summarize what we accomplished today"

```

### Recipe 3: Complex Refactoring

```
# 1. Use Opus for planning, Sonnet for execution
> /model opusplan

# 2. Plan the refactoring
> "Plan refactoring our authentication from sessions to JWT"

# 3. Review the plan
# (Claude presents approach, you approve or modify)

# 4. Execute with Sonnet
> "Execute the plan"

# 5. Verify nothing broke
> "Have an explore agent verify all auth-related tests pass"

# 6. Review the diff
> !git diff

```

### Recipe 4: PR Review Automation

```
# In CI/CD (GitHub Actions, etc.)
cat pr_diff.txt | claude -p "Review this PR diff for:" \
 --output-format json \
 --permission-mode plan \
 --max-turns 5 << EOF
- Security vulnerabilities
- Performance issues
- Code style violations
- Missing test coverage
Provide findings in JSON format.
EOF

```

### Recipe 5: Debugging Production Issues

```
# 1. Configure Sentry MCP for error access
claude --add-mcp sentry

# 2. Get error context
> "Fetch the last 10 errors from Sentry for the auth service"

# 3. Deep dive with thinking
> Alt+T # Enable extended thinking
> "Analyze these errors and identify the root cause"

# 4. Fix and verify
> "Implement a fix and add a regression test"
> !npm test

```

## Migration Guide

Coming from other AI coding tools? Here’s how concepts map.

### From GitHub Copilot

| Copilot Concept | Claude Code Equivalent |
| --- | --- |
| Tab completion | Not a primary focus: Claude Code does agentic editing |
| Chat + agentic CLI | REPL conversation with tool execution |
| Copilot instructions | CLAUDE.md + Skills |
| Specialized agents (Explore, Plan) | Subagents (Task tool) + agent teams |
| MCP support | 300+ MCP integrations + remote OAuth |
| ACP (Agent Client Protocol) | Hooks (12+ lifecycle events) |
| IDE + CLI | Terminal-native + IDE extensions |

What you gain: OS-level sandboxing, 22 hook lifecycle events, remote MCP with OAuth, subagent delegation with isolated context, and`--teleport` for cloud-to-local handoff. Copilot CLI (Jan 2026+) now has agentic capabilities — the comparison is feature differentiation, not a capability gap.

Cross-pollination (Feb 2026): Claude (Opus 4. and Sonnet 4.) is now available as a coding agent inside GitHub Copilot for Business and Pro subscribers at no extra cost — previously limited to Enterprise and Pro+ tiers. You can run Claude, Codex, and Copilot directly inside github.com, GitHub Mobile, and VS Code within existing GitHub workflows.

### From Cursor

| Cursor Concept | Claude Code Equivalent |
| --- | --- |
| Composer | Session with full tool access |
| Chat | REPL with subagent delegation |
| Codebase indexing | Real-time file access (Glob, Grep, Read) |
| Tab autocomplete | Not primary focus |
| IDE-integrated | Terminal-native + IDE extensions |
| MCP support (built-in) | MCP support (300+ integrations, remote OAuth) |
| Cursor rules (.mdc) + AGENTS.md | CLAUDE.md + Skills |
| Background agents | Remote agents (`&` prefix,`--teleport`) |

What you gain: 22 hook lifecycle events for automation, subagent delegation with isolated context, OS-level sandboxing (seatbelt/bubblewrap), remote MCP with native OAuth, and terminal-native workflow. Both tools support MCP — Claude Code differentiates with remote OAuth and tool search auto-deferral.

### From OpenAI Codex CLI

The most common comparison, and the question our GSC data confirms developers actually ask. Both are agentic coding CLIs from frontier labs, launched within months of each other. Here’s the direct matrix.

| Dimension | Claude Code | OpenAI Codex CLI |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation model | Opus 4. (Max/Team Premium default), Sonnet 4. (Pro/Team Standard/Enterprise/API default until Apr 23, 2026), Haiku 4. | GPT-5. (current default, released March 5, 2026, incorporates GPT-5.-Codex coding capability + native Computer Use); GPT-5.-Codex, GPT-5.-Codex, GPT-5.-Codex, GPT-5 available |
| Context window | 1M tokens on Opus 4. at standard pricing | 272K default / 1.05M long-context mode on GPT-5. (billed 2× input / 1.× output over 272K input); 400K input / 128K output on GPT-5.-Codex and GPT-5.-Codex |
| SWE-bench Verified | 87.% (Opus 4.) | 74.% (GPT-5-Codex baseline); GPT-5. not published on official page |
| SWE-bench Pro | 64.% (Opus 4.) | 57.% (GPT-5. official), 56.% (GPT-5.-Codex), 56.% (GPT-5.-Codex) |
| Terminal-Bench 2. | 69.% (Opus 4.) | 77.% (GPT-5.-Codex), 75.% (GPT-5. official), 64.% (GPT-5.-Codex) |
| Execution model | Local by default; optional cloud sandbox via`--teleport` | Local by default (Codex CLI); container-based sandbox via Codex Cloud |
| Sandboxing | OS-level (seatbelt on macOS, bubblewrap on Linux) | Local OS sandbox for Codex CLI; container isolation for Codex Cloud |
| Instruction file | `CLAUDE.md`(and`CLAUDE.local.md`) | `AGENTS.md` |
| Hook lifecycle | 22 events (PreToolUse, PostToolUse, UserPromptSubmit, Stop, SubagentStop, etc.) | Limited, mostly run-level hooks |
| Subagents / Agent Teams | Up to 10 parallel subagents, isolated context, background mode | Single-agent by default; parallel via spawning separate runs |
| MCP server ecosystem | 3,000+ MCP servers, remote OAuth, tool search | MCP support (newer), smaller ecosystem |
| Plugins | Plugin marketplace, plugin.json manifests, version pinning | No first-party plugin system yet |
| Pricing model | $5/$25 per MTok on Opus 4.; subscriptions $20–200/mo | Per-token on OpenAI API; ChatGPT Pro/Plus tiers bundle some Codex credits |
| Voice input | `/voice` push-to-talk (20 STT languages) | No CLI-native voice mode; ChatGPT apps have voice |
| Enterprise policy | Managed settings (`managed-settings.d/`), signed plugin marketplaces, CVE fast-track | Cloud-native policy via OpenAI admin |
| Windows support | Native binary + PowerShell tool | Codex CLI generally WSL-only |

When Claude Code wins on paper (today): on public benchmarks, Opus 4. leads SWE-bench Verified by 12. over the widely-cited GPT-5-Codex baseline and SWE-bench Pro by 6. over GPT-5.’s official 57.%. On Terminal-Bench 2., GPT-5.-Codex leads (77.%) followed by GPT-5. (75.% official), both ahead of Opus 4. (69.%). Numbers evolve with every model release, so check the latest vendor pages before making a multi-quarter bet.

When to pick Claude Code: you own a large codebase (1M context matters), you need local-first sandboxing with kernel-level isolation, you want deep hook-based automation (linters, policy checks, CI/CD gates), or you need native Windows support. SWE-bench Verified and Pro currently favor Opus 4. for production tasks.

When to pick Codex CLI: you’re already paying for ChatGPT Pro/Plus and want the same account, you prefer Codex Cloud’s container-based hermetic sandboxing for one-shot remote tasks, or your team standardizes on OpenAI infrastructure (Azure OpenAI, Foundry for Codex). Terminal-Bench 2. currently favors the Codex family for shell-heavy workflows (GPT-5.-Codex 77.%, GPT-5. 75.%).

Both, not either: many teams run both. Claude Code handles long-horizon refactors and agentic coding; Codex handles quick cloud-sandboxed tasks from ChatGPT. AGENTS.md and CLAUDE.md can coexist in the same repo (they serve different tools). See also the dedicated Codex vs Claude Code 2026 architecture comparison for deeper analysis.

Migration path (Codex → Claude Code): copy your`AGENTS.md` content into`CLAUDE.md`(Claude Code does not read`AGENTS.md`; you need the rename). Install Claude Code via the official installer, authenticate with`claude login`, then test with`claude --print "explain this repo"`. Most Codex patterns (file reads, shell commands, git operations) map 1:1.

### From ChatGPT / Claude Web

| Web Interface | Claude Code |
| --- | --- |
| Copy-paste code | Direct file access |
| Manual file creation | Writes files directly |
| No execution | Full bash, git, npm, etc. |
| Context resets | Session continuity (`-c` flag) |
| No codebase awareness | Glob, Grep, Read tools |
| No automation | Hooks, CI/CD integration |

What you gain: Everything. Web interfaces require copy-paste workflows; Claude Code operates directly on your codebase.

### Key Mindset Shifts

1. Stop copying code. Claude Code writes to files directly.
2. Stop context-switching. MCP brings external tools into the session.
3. Delegate aggressively. Subagents handle exploration without bloating your context.
4. Automate with hooks. Formatting, linting, logging happen automatically.
5. Think in sessions. Continue work with`-c`, not fresh conversations.

## Audience-Specific Guidance

For developers: - Haiku costs ~5x less than Opus; use it for subagents and simple tasks - Use`/compact` proactively during long sessions to manage context - Session IDs encoding context (`feature-${branch}-${timestamp}`) help locate previous work - Prefix common prompts with`#` to add to persistent memory without extra typing

For DevOps and platform teams: - Headless mode (`-p` flag) enables CI/CD integration with JSON output for parsing - Hooks guarantee execution regardless of model behavior; use for linting, formatting, security checks - MCP extends capabilities to databases, GitHub, Sentry, and 300+ integrations - Background tasks (`&` prefix) run on Claude Code Remote for asynchronous execution

For security teams: - Permission rules evaluated first-match-wins; structure allow/deny lists carefully - Sandbox mode isolates filesystem and network for untrusted codebases - Bash patterns match prefixes only, so`Bash(curl:*)` blocks all curl, not just specific URLs - Enterprise managed settings cannot be overridden by users

For team leads: - Skills auto-activate based on context; embed domain expertise without explicit invocation - Project skills commit to git; teammates get standardized expertise automatically - Cost tracking via`/cost` shows session breakdown; budget 10k-300k TPM per user based on team size - Custom commands in`.claude/commands/` create reusable workflows

For enterprise architects: - Managed settings deploy organization-wide policies via`/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`- AWS Bedrock, Google Vertex AI, and Microsoft Foundry integrate with existing cloud billing - Opus 4. with 1M context window enables processing entire codebases in a single prompt - Agent teams (v2..+) enable parallel multi-agent coordination for complex tasks - Start pilot with 5-10 developers before full rollout

## Changelog

The reference is a living document. Updates are applied as Claude Code evolves.

| Date | Change | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-07-25 | Guide v2.: CLI v2..–v2.. + Claude Opus 5. Claude Opus 5 (`claude-opus-5`) is the new default Opus and what`/model opus` selects (v2..): 1M context, 128K max output (300K on Batches with the`output-300k-2026-03-24` beta), $5/$25 per MTok — identical to Opus 4., adaptive thinking,`effort` defaulting to`high` on both the Claude API and Claude Code across the`low`/`medium`/`high`/`xhigh`/`max` ladder, reliable knowledge cutoff May 2026. Default model on Claude Max, strongest on Claude Pro; Opus 4. moves to the docs’ Legacy models table. Two breaking changes vs Opus 4.: thinking is now on by default for requests with no`thinking` field, and`thinking: {type: "disabled"}` combined with effort`xhigh` or`max` returns a 400 (validated per request). See Which Model Should I Choose. CORRECTION — Fast Mode: this guide previously described fast mode as research-preview Opus 4.-only at $30/$150 (6× base) and unavailable on Opus 4.. As of v2..,`/fast` applies to Opus 5 and Opus 4. at $10/$50 per MTok (2× base) for up to 2.× output tokens per second; Opus 4. was removed from fast mode and`claude-opus-4-7`+`speed: "fast"` now errors rather than falling back to standard speed (Opus 4. remains the silent-fallback exception). Still research preview, Claude API only — not Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry/Claude Platform on AWS — with its own rate limit, and switching speeds invalidates the prompt cache. Section rewritten. CORRECTION — subagent nesting: the v2./v2. text said nested spawning was off by default (v2..). v2.. reverses that again: subagents spawn nested subagents up to depth 3 by default;`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH=1` disables nesting. The default has now moved 5 → 1 → 3 in six weeks, so pin it explicitly rather than inheriting it (see Subagents + env-var reference). New`DirectoryAdded` hook event — fires after`/add-dir` or the SDK`register_repo_root` control request registers a new working directory mid-session (see Hook Events). New`sandbox.network.strictAllowlist` setting — denies non-allowlisted hosts for sandboxed commands without prompting (see Sandbox Mode). Dynamic workflows now default to a medium size guideline (“aim for fewer than 15 agents”) with a new`workflowSizeGuideline` settings key settable from any settings file; the`/config` row hides while one is set, and a running workflow’s status line shows the current size. Nested subagent forwarding in stream-json — depth-2+ subagents now surface with`--forward-subagent-text`, keyed by their spawning Agent`tool_use` id. Changelog-only:`mcp_server_errors` added to the headless stream-json init event listing`--mcp-config` entries skipped by config validation (terminal runs print a startup warning); managed MCP allowlist/denylist`${VAR}` entries resolve from the startup environment and managed-settings env instead of settings-file env;`/model` picker shows the merged “Opus (1M context)” row instead of plain “Opus” and highlights only the newest model’s name; Fable row no longer shows a stale “Requires usage credits” label on plans that include it;`claude -p` no longer drops text it already produced when a turn dies on a mid-stream API error;`claude --teleport` names the repo your checkout points at on a mismatch; the`/claude-api` skill defaults to Opus 5 with a 4. migration path; Remote Control stale fast-mode status after switch/reconnect/failed org check fixed and the “only available via api.anthropic.com” error now names the setting that caused it;`CLAUDE_CODE_GIT_BASH_PATH` pointing at a non-bash binary is ignored with a warning instead of exiting; GNU screen copy-on-select no longer prints base64; Vim NORMAL-mode ← on an empty prompt returns to the agent view; screen-reader mode echoes only the typed character instead of rewriting the input line. v2.. (July 25) is bug fixes and reliability improvements only. Also corrected: the 2026-07-24 row credited the MCP HTTP-status/whitespace-warning item to v2.. — it shipped in v2... v2.. is the latest release. | 196197198 |
| 2026-07-24 | Guide v2.: CLI v2...`/code-review` now runs as a background subagent — review work no longer fills your conversation; stacked slash commands stay the review target (see Slash Commands).`context: fork` skills run in the background by default — opt out per skill with the new`background: false` frontmatter key (see Skills Frontmatter Reference). Auto-mode dialogs thinned: the dangerous-`rm`, background-`&`, and suspicious-Windows-path checks no longer open permission dialogs — the auto-mode classifier adjudicates them; plan mode with auto no longer prompts for Bash commands the static analyzer can’t prove read-only — the classifier judges them (see Auto Mode). Security: agent frontmatter hooks no longer run from untrusted folders — the agent file’s own folder must have accepted workspace trust (see Component-Scoped Hooks).`/deep-research` is manual-invoke only — Claude no longer launches it on its own, matching v2..’s`/verify`/`/code-review` rule. Changelog-only:`claude mcp list` and`/mcp` show HTTP status + error text on failed connects, plus a warning for MCP config values with hidden leading/trailing whitespace (correction 2026-07-25: this item shipped in v2.., not v2.. — it was credited to the wrong release here); left-arrow presses right after editing now confirm before discarding the conversation, and Esc in the agent view returns to the conversation it backgrounded; server-managed settings — benign feature/cost toggles skip the settings-approval prompt; agent names containing`:` rejected (reserved for plugin namespacing); skill/plugin frontmatter booleans accept`yes`/`no`/`on`/`off`/`1`/`0`; trust dialogs name the repository root the grant covers; improved sandbox command restrictions for IDE interactions;`Ctrl+B` backgrounding applies the standard background-shell caps; Bedrock application-inference-profile ARNs metered at the configured model’s rates; fork-session lineage preserved after compaction in headless/SDK sessions; Windows`\u`-path corruption fix; remote sessions stop sending zombie heartbeats after their worker is replaced. v2.. was the latest release when this row was written. | 195196 |
| 2026-07-22 | Guide v2.: CLI v2... Subagent-nesting default reversed: subagents no longer spawn nested subagents by default — set`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH` to allow deeper nesting. This retires v2..’s “recursive sub-agents up to 5 levels deep” as default behavior (recursion was the default v2..–v2..; see Subagents + env-var reference). New concurrency cap: at most 20 concurrently-running subagents by default (`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS`) so one message can’t fan out unbounded background agents — distinct from the v2.. per-session spawn total (`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION`, 200/session).`--max-budget-usd` now stops background subagents: once the cap is reached, new spawns are denied and running background agents are halted (previously background agents escaped the budget). Emoji shortcode autocomplete in the prompt input —`:heart:`→ ❤️, partials suggest — disable with`emojiCompletionEnabled: false`. Changelog-only: background-session isolation canonicalizes symlinked working directories (no more workspace-folder escape); managed`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` now governs all signals (lower-scope signal-specific overrides can no longer redirect telemetry); warnings when transcript writes fail (e.g. disk full) or session saving is off via inherited env; MCP truncated-output memory leak fixed; Windows auto-update restores the preserved`claude.exe` after failed updates; Bedrock Opus 4. auto-compact/`/compact` fix; corporate mTLS/TLS-verify/OAuth-scope/proxy settings honored in Claude Desktop sessions;`--resume`/`/resume` TypeError on malformed attachment entries; unstoppable background shells after`/background` or exit on loaded machines; brace-expansion OOM at startup now budget-bounded; Remote Control late-joiners now see pending permission prompts; screen-reader startup-announcement and thinking-row re-render fixes; footer PR badges clickable over ssh/tmux (`FORCE_HYPERLINK=0` opts out); login-expiry warning at 3 days (was 5); frontend-design plugin tip capped at 3 lifetime impressions. v2.. is the latest release. | 194 |
| 2026-07-21 | Guide v2.: CLI v2..–v2.. (v2.. was never released — same skip pattern as 2../189/192/). v2.. — permission-scoping change: single-segment`dir/**` allow rules (e.g.`Edit(src/**)`) now match only`<cwd>/dir`, no longer any`dir/` anywhere in the tree — write`**/dir/**` for any-depth;`deny`/`ask` rules keep any-depth matching, and hook`if:` conditions get the same cwd scoping (see Permission Rules Syntax + Matchers). Fail-closed hardening batch: Windows PowerShell 5. bypass fixed; Bash fd-redirect forms fail closed; commands >10,000 chars always prompt; zsh subscripts/modifiers in`[[ ]]` prompt; unsafe`help`/`man` variants no longer auto-approved; remote-session prompts can’t proceed before local confirmation;`docker`/Podman daemon-redirect flags (`--url`,`--connection`,`--identity`, remote mode) prompt;`file -m`/`--magic-file`/`-f`/`--files-from` requires permission. New EndConversation tool — Claude can end sessions on highly abusive input or jailbreak attempts, as on claude.ai since 2025. SessionStart hooks report source`"fork"` for forked sessions (was`"resume"`). OTel:`CLAUDE_CODE_OTEL_CONTENT_MAX_LENGTH` configures the 60 KB content-truncation limit; log events gain`message.uuid`/`client_request_id`/`tool_source`;`subagentStatusLine` payload gains reasoning effort. Changelog-only: settings files >2 MiB fail at startup with a clear error; ISO`modified` timestamp in memory frontmatter; progress heartbeat for long-silent tool calls; stream-json exit-drain scaling fix; hook exit-code-2 now blocks as documented when stdout JSON fails schema validation;`--settings` plugin-loading regression (since v2..) fixed; background daemon/session lifecycle cleanup;`pkill -f` self-match fix. v2..: Claude no longer auto-runs the`/verify` and`/code-review` skills — explicit invocation only. v2..: new`sandbox.filesystem.disabled` setting — skip filesystem isolation, keep network egress control (see Sandbox Mode). Changelog-only: quadratic message-normalization slowdown fixed; auto-mode HTTP 401 after OAuth rotation fixed; worktree-isolated subagents can no longer redirect git to the shared checkout (`git -C`/`GIT_DIR`);`.claude` symlink write-redirect fixed;`/rewind` refuses symlink/hardlink restore paths;`/context` warns on context-window overflow; mid-session skill/command edits appear in the slash menu without restart;`/fork` confirmation shows session name +`claude attach` id; cloud-session mid-turn container-restart recovery. v2.. is the latest release. | |

| 2026-06-02 | Guide v2.: CLI v2... Security — edit-becomes-execution gating:`acceptEdits` now prompts before writing shell startup files (`.zshenv`,`.zlogin`,`.bash_login`),`~/.config/git/`, and build-tool configs that grant code execution (`.npmrc`,`.yarnrc*`,`bunfig.toml`,`.bazelrc`,`.pre-commit-config.yaml`,`.devcontainer/`, etc.) — extending the existing`.claude/`/`.git/`/`.vscode/` write protections to the wider class of files where an edit turns the next shell/install/commit into an execution vector. Workflow keyword rename: the dynamic-workflow trigger keyword changed from`workflow` to`ultracode`(highlighted violet in the prompt input); the word “workflow” no longer triggers a run, though asking for one in your own words still works. Edit ergonomics: a single-file`grep`/`egrep`/`fgrep` now satisfies the read-before-edit check, so Edit no longer requires a separate Read after viewing a file via grep. Removed:`CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE` is now a no-op (fully removed); the JetBrains plugin install suggestion is gone from startup. Plus ~25 fixes: WSL copy-on-select via PowerShell interop (not OSC 52),`claude agents` session restore dropping chat history / re-running the original prompt, overnight-retired background sessions losing conversation,`claude --bg`“socket missing” cold-start race, Windows background-session input unresponsiveness under load, terminal sync-output artifacts on Apple Terminal/tmux, CJK IME caret position in`claude agents`,`file:///C:/...` link rewriting on Windows, voice mode with non-ASCII project/branch names, the auto-mode-unavailable message on Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry now correctly pointing to`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE`,`/effort ultracode` no longer offered on models that can’t run xhigh, and SIGTERM-before-SIGKILL on background-session teardown so cleanup handlers run. | 2026-04-24 | Guide v2.: CLI v2..–v2.. + April 23 quality postmortem.`/usage` merges`/cost` and`/stats` into a single dialog (both names remain as typing shortcuts). New managed-settings policy:`wslInheritsWindowsSettings` lets WSL inherit Windows-side managed settings.`cleanupPeriodDays` retention sweep now also covers`~/.claude/tasks/`,`~/.claude/shell-snapshots/`, and`~/.claude/backups/`. Advisor Tool is now experimental (label, learn-more link, no longer gets stuck after errors).`claude plugin tag` creates release git tags with version validation;`blockedMarketplaces`/`strictKnownMarketplaces` now enforced on every plugin install/update path including`hostPattern`/`pathPattern`. Security: sandbox auto-allow no longer bypasses dangerous-path checks for`rm`/`rmdir` on`/`,`$HOME`, and other critical system roots. Postmortem (April 23): Anthropic published a postmortem covering three quality regressions resolved by April 20 — (1) reasoning effort default downgraded to`medium` March 4–April 7 (reverted), (2) prompt cache thinking-clear bug March 26–April 10 (fixed in v2..), (3) system-prompt verbosity constraint April 16–20 (~3% coding quality drop on Opus 4. / Opus 4.; smaller drop on Sonnet 4.; reverted in v2..). Usage limits reset for all subscribers April 23. Auto mode for Claude Code. Anthropic, March 24, 2026. Auto Mode: classifier-based permission system where a separate Sonnet 4. model reviews each action before execution. Safer alternative to –dangerously-skip-permissions. Team plan first, Enterprise and API following. ↩↩↩

Claude Code Auto Mode Documentation. Auto Mode architecture: read-only and working-dir edits auto-approved, custom rules resolve first, classifier evaluates remaining actions. Auto-blocked: curl|bash, force-push main, production deploys, mass cloud deletions, IAM changes, external data exfiltration. Circuit breaker: 3 consecutive or 20 total blocks pauses to manual. ↩↩↩

(`ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H`, Session Recap/`/recap`, model can invoke built-in slash commands via Skill tool,`/undo` alias,`/model` mid-conversation warning). v2.. (extended-thinking indicator). v2.. (`/tui`/`/tui fullscreen`, push notifications tool for Remote Control,`/focus` separated from`Ctrl+O`,`autoScrollEnabled`,`--resume`/`--continue` resurrects scheduled tasks,`/context`/`/exit`/`/reload-plugins` from Remote Control clients, Session Recap for telemetry-disabled users,`TRACEPARENT`/`TRACESTATE` distributed tracing, Write tool informs model when user edited diff, security hardening around PermissionRequest hooks and open-in-editor). v2.. (Opus 4. launch support,`xhigh` effort,`/effort` interactive slider, Auto Mode for Max without flag,`/less-permission-prompts`,`/ultrareview`, “Auto (match terminal)” theme, Windows PowerShell tool via`CLAUDE_CODE_USE_POWERSHELL_TOOL`, read-only Bash permission reduction with glob patterns,`/setup-vertex` and`/setup-bedrock` improvements,`OTEL_LOG_RAW_API_BODIES`). v2.. (“temporarily unavailable” hotfix for Opus 4. in Auto Mode). See also: GitHub blog on Opus 4. GA, Cyber Verification Program form. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code — Model Configuration. Definitive source for per-plan defaults (Max/Team Premium → Opus 4.; Pro/Team Standard/Enterprise/API → Sonnet 4.; Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry → Sonnet 4.; Enterprise + API change to Opus 4. on April 23, 2026). Covers`/effort` levels (`low`/`medium`/`high`/`xhigh`/`max` for Opus 4.;`low`/`medium`/`high`/`max` for Opus 4. and Sonnet 4.), effort fallback behavior, adaptive reasoning (Opus 4. uses adaptive only;`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ADAPTIVE_THINKING=1` reverts 4. to fixed budgets), and extended context rules per plan (Max/Team/Enterprise: Opus 1M included; Pro: via extra usage; API: full access). Opus 4. requires Claude Code v2..+. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Anthropic — Pricing. Canonical pricing reference (April 2026). Base input/output rates: Opus 4. and 4. at $5/$25 per MTok; Sonnet 4. at $3/$15; Haiku 4. at $1/$5. Prompt caching multipliers: 5-min cache write 1.×, 1-hour cache write 2×, cache hit 0.× base input price. Long-context: “Claude Mythos Preview, Opus 4., Opus 4., and Sonnet 4. include the full 1M token context window at standard pricing” — no long-context premium on any current-generation model. Data residency (`inference_geo="us"`): 1.× multiplier on all token categories for Opus 4. and newer. Batch API: 50% discount. ↩↩↩

Superseded — retained for the Opus 4. era only; see 198 for current fast-mode facts. Claude Code — Fast Mode, as documented February 2026. Research-preview feature. Opus 4.-only — “Fast mode is not available on Opus 4. or other models.” Provides ~2.× faster output at 6× base pricing ($30 input / $150 output per MTok). Enabling`/fast` automatically switches session to Opus 4.; disabling leaves session on Opus 4.. Not available on Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry. Requires extra usage; Team/Enterprise require admin enablement. Disable entirely with`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_FAST_MODE=1`. ↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG. v2.. (April 17, 2026): CLI spawns a native Claude Code binary via per-platform optional dependency instead of bundled JavaScript;`sandbox.network.deniedDomains` blocks specific domains even when`allowedDomains` wildcards would permit them; fullscreen`Shift+↑`/`Shift+↓` scrolls the viewport when extending a selection past the visible edge;`Ctrl+A`/`Ctrl+E` move to the start/end of the current logical line in multiline input (readline-style); Windows`Ctrl+Backspace` deletes the previous word;`/loop` Esc cancels pending wakeups and shows “Claude resuming /loop wakeup” messages;`/extra-usage` now works from Remote Control (mobile/web) clients;`/ultrareview` faster launch with parallelized checks, diffstat in the launch dialog, and animated launching state; subagents that stall mid-stream fail with a clear error after 10 minutes; Bash multi-line commands whose first line is a comment show the full command in the transcript (UI-spoofing fix);`cd <current-directory> && git …` no longer prompts on no-ops; macOS`Bash(rm:*)` allow rules treat`/private/{etc,var,tmp,home}` as dangerous removal targets; Bash deny rules match commands wrapped in`env`/`sudo`/`watch`/`ionice`/`setsid` and similar wrappers;`Bash(find:*)` allow rules no longer auto-approve`find -exec`/`-delete`; Opus 4. via Bedrock Application Inference Profile ARN no longer returns`thinking.type.enabled is not supported` 400; plus fixes for MCP concurrent-call timeout handling, markdown tables with pipe-containing inline code, session recap auto-firing during composition, Remote Control subagent streaming and archival, SDK image-block failures degrading to text, and`dangerouslyDisableSandbox` bypassing permission prompts. v2.. (April 18, 2026): single fix for a crash in the permission dialog when an agent-teams teammate requested tool permission. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Codex vs Claude Code benchmark and capability sources (April 2026). Opus 4. numbers from Anthropic: Introducing Claude Opus 4.: 87.% SWE-bench Verified, 64.% SWE-bench Pro, 69.% Terminal-Bench 2.. GPT-5. specs from OpenAI GPT-5. model docs: released March 5, 2026 (snapshot`gpt-5.-2026-03-05`), 272K default context / 1.05M long-context mode, 128K max output, $2. input / $0. cached / $15 output per MTok, 2×/1.× long-context multiplier over 272K input. GPT-5. coding benchmarks (official) from Introducing GPT-5.: 57.% SWE-bench Pro, 75.% Terminal-Bench 2.; SWE-bench Verified is NOT published on the official page. GPT-5-Codex baseline SWE-bench Verified 74.% from OpenAI’s original Codex launch materials. GPT-5.-Codex and GPT-5.-Codex numbers from OpenAI: Introducing GPT-5.-Codex: GPT-5.-Codex 56.% SWE-bench Pro / 64.% Terminal-Bench 2.; GPT-5.-Codex 56.% SWE-bench Pro / 77.% Terminal-Bench 2.. GPT-5.-Codex context window (400K / 128K max output) from OpenAI model docs for gpt-5.-codex. Codex CLI execution model (local by default) from OpenAI Codex CLI help; Codex Cloud container sandboxing is separate. Claude Code instruction files (`CLAUDE.md`+`CLAUDE.local.md`, no`AGENTS.md` support) from Anthropic memory docs. Competitor comparison formats cross-checked against Builder.io: Codex vs Claude Code and DataCamp: Codex vs Claude Code. ↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG— composite reference for v2.. (April 20, 2026), v2.. (April 22, 2026), v2.. (April 23, 2026), and v2.. (April 23, 2026). v2..:`/resume` 67% faster on 40MB+ sessions; faster MCP startup with multiple stdio servers (`resources/templates/list` deferred to first`@`-mention); inline thinking spinner (“still thinking”, “thinking more”, “almost done thinking”);`/config` search now matches option values;`/doctor` opens during a turn; plugin auto-install missing dependencies; Bash tool surfaces`gh` GitHub API rate-limit hint; Settings Usage tab shows immediately and survives rate-limited usage endpoint; agent frontmatter`hooks:` fire when running as main-thread agent via`--agent`; security fix — sandbox auto-allow no longer bypasses dangerous-path check for`rm`/`rmdir` targeting`/`,`$HOME`, or other critical roots. (The April 16 system-prompt verbosity constraint that v2.. reverted is documented in the postmortem at 153 rather than the v2.. release notes.) v2..:`CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT=1` for forked subagents on external builds; agent frontmatter`mcpServers` load for main-thread agent sessions via`--agent`;`/model` selections persist across restarts even when project pins different model and startup header shows pin source;`/resume` offers stale-session summary; concurrent MCP startup (local + claude.ai) by default;`blockedMarketplaces`/`strictKnownMarketplaces` enforced on plugin install/update/refresh/autoupdate; Advisor Tool experimental with label, learn-more link, startup notification when enabled;`cleanupPeriodDays` retention sweep covers`~/.claude/tasks/`,`~/.claude/shell-snapshots/`,`~/.claude/backups/`; OTel`user_prompt` events include`command_name`/`command_source`(custom/MCP names redacted unless`OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS=1`);`cost.usage`/`token.usage`/`api_request`/`api_error` include`effort` attribute; native macOS/Linux builds replace`Glob`/`Grep` with embedded`bfs`/`ugrep` via the Bash tool; default effort for Pro/Max on Sonnet 4. and Opus 4. is now`high`(was`medium`); fix — Opus 4. sessions were computing`/context` against 200K window instead of native 1M, causing premature autocompact. v2..: vim visual mode (`v`) and visual-line mode (`V`) with selection, operators, visual feedback;`/cost` and`/stats` merged into`/usage`(typing shortcuts retained); named themes via`/theme` or`~/.claude/themes/*.json`(plugins ship`themes/` directory); hooks invoke MCP tools directly via`type: "mcp_tool"`;`DISABLE_UPDATES` env var blocks all update paths including manual`claude update`(stricter than`DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`); WSL inherits Windows-side managed settings via`wslInheritsWindowsSettings` policy;`$defaults` sentinel in`autoMode.allow`/`soft_deny`/`environment` adds custom rules alongside built-in list; “Don’t ask again” auto mode opt-in;`claude plugin tag` creates release git tags with version validation. v2..:`/config` settings (theme, editor mode, verbose) persist to`~/.claude/settings.json` with project/local/policy precedence;`prUrlTemplate` setting points footer PR badge at custom code-review URL instead of github.com;`CLAUDE_CODE_HIDE_CWD` hides working directory in startup logo;`--from-pr` accepts GitLab MR, Bitbucket PR, GitHub Enterprise PR URLs;`--print` honors agent`tools:`/`disallowedTools:` frontmatter;`--agent <name>` honors`permissionMode` for built-in agents; PowerShell tool commands auto-approve in permission mode (matches Bash);`PostToolUse`/`PostToolUseFailure` hook inputs include`duration_ms`(tool execution time, excluding permission prompts and PreToolUse hooks); subagent and SDK MCP server reconfiguration connects servers in parallel; security —`blockedMarketplaces` correctly enforces`hostPattern` and`pathPattern` entries; OTel`tool_result` and`tool_decision` events include`tool_use_id`;`tool_result` includes`tool_input_size_bytes`; status line stdin JSON includes`effort.level` and`thinking.enabled`; tool search disabled by default on Vertex AI (opt in with`ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH`). ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

An update on recent Claude Code quality reports. Anthropic engineering postmortem, April 23, 2026. Covers three independent quality regressions resolved by April 20 (v2..), with usage limits reset for all subscribers on April 23: (1) Reasoning effort default — changed from`high` to`medium` on March 4, 2026 to reduce UI freezing; reverted April 7 after operator feedback that users preferred higher intelligence over UI smoothness. Affected Sonnet 4. and Opus 4.. (2) Prompt cache thinking-clear bug — a cache optimization meant to clear thinking content once per idle session instead cleared it on every turn for the rest of the session, causing forgetfulness, repetition, and faster usage limit drain. Active March 26 to April 10, fixed in v2... Affected Sonnet 4. and Opus 4.. (3) System prompt verbosity constraint — added April 16 to handle Opus 4.’s chattiness; per Anthropic, the ~3% coding-quality drop was measured on Opus 4. and Opus 4. (Sonnet 4. was affected but with a smaller drop); reverted April 20 in v2... Anthropic’s stated mitigations: broader pre-release evaluation suites, tighter system-prompt change controls, mandatory code review on intelligence-affecting changes, and gradual rollouts. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code release notes — composite reference for v2..(April 28, 2026, 00:31 UTC) and v2..(April 28, 2026, 22:05 UTC). v2..:`alwaysLoad` MCP server-config option (skip tool-search deferral); MCP startup auto-retry 3x;`claude plugin prune` and`--prune` cascade; type-to-filter`/skills`; PostToolUse`hookSpecificOutput.updatedToolOutput` extended from MCP-only to all tools;`.claude/skills/`,`.claude/agents/`,`.claude/commands/` exempt from`--dangerously-skip-permissions` prompt; Vertex AI X. cert-based Workload Identity Federation (mTLS ADC); SDK`mcp_authenticate``redirectUri`; OTel adds`stop_reason`,`gen_ai.response.finish_reasons`, and`user_system_prompt`(gated behind`OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS`) to LLM-request spans;`/terminal-setup` enables iTerm2 clipboard access for`/copy` from tmux; tab title in`language` setting;`CLAUDE_CODE_FORK_SUBAGENT=1` extended to non-interactive`claude -p`; faster post-upgrade startup (Recent Activity panel removed from release-notes splash); claude.ai connector deduplication against same upstream URL. v2..:`ANTHROPIC_BEDROCK_SERVICE_TIER` env var (`default`/`flex`/`priority`) sent as`X-Amzn-Bedrock-Service-Tier`;`/resume` PR URL search across GitHub, GHE, GitLab, Bitbucket;`/mcp` shows hidden connectors with remove-duplicate hint and clarifies post-browser unauthorized message; OTel`claude_code.at_mention` log event for`@`-mention resolution; numeric attributes on`api_request`/`api_error` emitted as numbers (was strings). High-signal fixes across both: ~2GB memory leak in`/usage`, multi-GB RSS growth on image-heavy sessions,`--resume` corrupt-transcript-line skip + large external-build session crash fix, Bash recovery when CWD deleted/moved, Bedrock thinking-on-application-inference-profile fix, Vertex/Bedrock`output_config` extra-input rejection on structured output, ToolSearch missing nonblocking MCP tools that connected after session start, image-resize ceiling 2576→2000 px on newer models,`!exit`/`!quit` in bash mode no longer kills CLI, scrollback duplication on tmux/GNOME/Windows Terminal/Konsole, malformed`hooks` entry no longer invalidates the entire`settings.json`. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. April 29, 2026, 03:29 UTC. Single bug fix: “Fixed OAuth authentication failing with a 401 retry loop when`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_EXPERIMENTAL_BETAS=1` is set.” ↩

Composite latest-line verification for May 6, 2026: Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code CHANGELOG, and NPM package metadata for @anthropic-ai/claude-code. v2.. added gateway model discovery for`/model`,`claude project purge`, OAuth code paste fallback, skill activation OTel, PowerShell primary-shell behavior on Windows, and changed`--dangerously-skip-permissions` protected-path prompting. v2.. added zip support to`--plugin-dir`, MCP tool-count and zero-tool visibility, console-auth`--channels`, fixed`EnterWorktree`, and repaired plugin/cache/MCP/session issues. v2.. added`--plugin-url`,`CLAUDE_CODE_FORCE_SYNC_OUTPUT`,`CLAUDE_CODE_PACKAGE_MANAGER_AUTO_UPDATE`,`skillOverrides`, opt-in gateway model discovery via`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_GATEWAY_MODEL_DISCOVERY=1`, and the plugin manifest`themes`/`monitors` validation warning. v2.. fixed VS Code extension activation on Windows and Mantle endpoint authentication; the release notes did not announce additional command or configuration additions. ↩↩↩↩↩

Higher usage limits for Claude and a compute deal with SpaceX. Anthropic, May 6, 2026. Three changes: (1) Claude Code five-hour rate limits doubled for Pro, Max, Team, and seat-based Enterprise plans; (2) peak-hours reduction removed for Pro and Max Claude Code accounts; (3) API rate limits for Claude Opus models raised “considerably,” with a per-tier table on the announcement page. Compute backstop: Anthropic gets “all of the compute capacity at their Colossus 1 data center” — “more than 300 megawatts of new capacity (over 220,000 NVIDIA GPUs) within the month.” Anthropic also expressed interest in developing “multiple gigawatts of orbital AI compute capacity” with SpaceX. ↩↩

Agents for financial services. Anthropic, May 5, 2026. Ten ready-to-run agent templates: pitch builder, earnings reviewer, KYC screener, month-end closer, and six more. Each ships as a plugin in Claude Cowork and Claude Code, and as a cookbook for Claude Managed Agents. Microsoft 365 add-ins: Claude in Excel, PowerPoint, and Word with cross-application context; Outlook integration coming soon. Data connector partnerships with Dun & Bradstreet, Fiscal AI, Financial Modeling Prep, Guidepoint, IBISWorld, SS&C IntraLinks, Third Bridge, and Verisk. Moody’s MCP app: credit ratings and data on more than 600 million companies, accessible within Claude. Claude Cowork Dispatch: voice and text task assignment for analysts. Opus 4. cited at 64.% on Vals AI Finance Agent benchmark. ↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. May 6, 2026. Adds`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` env var to the Bash tool subprocess (matches the`session_id` passed to hooks). Adds`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_ALTERNATE_SCREEN=1` env var to opt out of the fullscreen alternate-screen renderer and keep the conversation in the terminal’s native scrollback. Updates the`/tui fullscreen` startup banner to describe additional renderer benefits (lower memory usage, mouse support, auto-copy on select). Plus 20+ fixes spanning terminal/input handling (SIGINT, SSH disconnects, terminal modes, cursor positioning), text editing and pasting (emoji and grapheme corruption, vim operators, escape sequences, swallowed text after paste-starts-with-`/`), UI/rendering (fullscreen blank screen after sleep, scroll speed, scroll-wheel handling, autocomplete popup), session management (`--resume` failures, emoji truncation, plan-mode`--permission-mode` flag), command utilities (`/usage`,`/terminal-setup`,`/effort`,`/status`,`/login`,`/upgrade`), MCP server integration (tool fetching, authorization display, non-protocol data, unbounded memory growth in stdio MCP servers,`tools/list` retry), platform-specific issues (Windows Terminal, JetBrains IDE, macOS keyboards, X Linux), and Bedrock+Vertex 400 errors when`ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H` is set. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. May 7, 2026. Adds`worktree.baseRef` setting (`fresh`|`head`) — and per the release notes: “the default`fresh` changes`EnterWorktree`’s base back to`origin/<default>`(it has been local`HEAD` since 2..) — set`worktree.baseRef: \"head\"` to keep unpushed commits in new worktrees.” Hooks now receive the active effort level via the`effort.level` JSON input field and the`$CLAUDE_EFFORT` env var; Bash tool commands can also read`$CLAUDE_EFFORT`. Adds`sandbox.bwrapPath` and`sandbox.socatPath` managed settings (Linux/WSL) for custom bubblewrap and socat binary locations. Adds`parentSettingsBehavior` admin-tier key (`'first-wins' | 'merge'`) to opt SDK`managedSettings`(parent tier) into the policy merge. Fixes subagents not discovering project, user, or plugin skills via the`Skill` tool. Other fixes: parallel-session 401-after-refresh-token race, drive-root allow-rule scoping, MCP OAuth proxy and mTLS support, Remote Control stop/interrupt completing the cancel, cross-session`/effort` leakage,`--remote-control` listed in`--help`. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Collaborate with Claude across Excel, PowerPoint, Word and Outlook. Anthropic, May 7, 2026. Claude for Excel, PowerPoint, and Word are generally available; Claude for Outlook is in public beta for all paid plans. Cross-application reactivity (“adjust an assumption in Excel and the chart in PowerPoint and the number in your Word memo automatically update, too”). Outlook adds inbox triage (“Claude…sorts messages by what needs your response, what it can draft for you, and what’s noise”). Available to all Mac and Windows users on paid plans. ↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. through v2.. release notes. v2..(May 9, 2026): “[VSCode] Fixed extension failing to activate on Windows.” v2..(May 9, 2026): “Internal fixes.” v2..(May 11, 2026): Agent View (`claude agents`),`/goal`, command-hook`args: string[]`,`continueOnBlock` for`PostToolUse`,`CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` for MCP stdio servers,`${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}` interpolation in plugin commands, OpenTelemetry active-time fix in`--print`, and related reliability fixes. See also: Agent View docs and Goal docs. ↩↩↩↩
