Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 10–11, 2026. v2.. (June 10): sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents, up to 5 levels deep (recursive delegation); when`AWS_REGION` is unset, the AWS Bedrock region is read from`~/.aws` and`/status` shows the resolved source;`/plugin` gains a marketplace search bar; the`claude_code.lines_of_code.count` OTEL metric adds a`model` attribute. Fixes: stuck sessions on 1M context without credits, image-processing errors, a background sub-agent stuck state, model picker/restrictions, and team-memory recall. v2.. (June 11, UTC): Fable 5 model names carrying a`[1m]` suffix are automatically normalized/stripped (Fable 5 already includes 1M context by default, so the suffix — meaningful only on Opus/Sonnet — is redundant); plus a Windows fix for a spurious “sandbox dependencies missing” startup warning. Verification: GitHub releases`v2..` and`v2..` and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 12, 2026. v2..:`wheelScrollAccelerationEnabled` setting (disable mouse-wheel scroll acceleration in fullscreen mode);`/model` picker shows the family the Default resolves to as its own row (Opus on Max/Team Premium/Enterprise and pay-as-you-go API, Sonnet on Pro/Team);`/model` picker no longer shows a hardcoded Sonnet label when`ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL` pins a different Sonnet; VSCode Account & usage dialog (`/usage`) adds attribution showing cache misses, long context, subagents, and per-skill/agent/plugin/MCP breakdowns over the last 24h or 7d; fixes for Bedrock GovCloud (`us-gov-*`) inference-profile prefix deriving`global` instead of`us-gov`, background sessions inheriting another session’s`ANTHROPIC_*` provider env, git commit co-author model-name attribution,`/advisor` pre-selecting a blocked advisor model, skill hot-reload re-sending the entire listing, and the Fable 5 “consuming usage credits” banner showing for enterprise usage-billing accounts. v2..:`enforceAvailableModels` managed setting — when enabled, the`availableModels` allowlist also constrains the Default model (a Default resolving to a disallowed model falls back to the first allowed model), and user/project settings can no longer widen a managed`availableModels` list. v2..: session titles generated in the language of your conversation (set`language` to pin a specific language);`footerLinksRegexes` setting for regex-matched link badges in the footer row (user or managed settings); Bedrock credentials from`awsCredentialExport` cached until their`Expiration` instead of a fixed hour;`availableModels` enforcement hardened so alias picks can’t redirect to a blocked model via`ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_*_MODEL` and`/fast` refuses to switch to a model outside the allowlist; auto mode falls back from Fable 5 to the best available Opus for orgs without Opus 4.; hook`if` conditions for Read/Edit/Write tool paths (`Edit(src/**)`,`Read(~/.ssh/**)`,`Read(.env)`) now match correctly; Linux sandbox symlinked-settings start fix;`/copy` and mouse-selection copy reaching the system clipboard inside tmux over SSH;`/cd` and worktree moves no longer reporting the previous directory’s git branch; plus numerous Remote Control,`claude agents`, and background-session fixes. Current-session verification June 15, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases`v2..`–`v2..` and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and v2.. release notes, plus the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 15–16, 2026. v2..:`Tool(param:value)` permission-rule syntax matching tool input parameters with a`*` wildcard (e.g.`Agent(model:opus)`); skills in nested`.claude/skills` load when working on files there, with`<dir>:<name>` disambiguation on name clash; nested`.claude/` agents, workflows, and output-styles resolve closest-to-cwd on collision (project-scope workflow saves target the closest existing`.claude/workflows/`); auto mode evaluates subagent spawns with the classifier before launch; compaction now honors the`--fallback-model` chain on overload/availability errors; MCP server-level specs (`mcp__server`,`mcp__server__*`,`mcp__*`) in subagent`disallowedTools` no longer silently ignored;`/doctor` flat-tree layout;`/bug` requires a description; the workflow prompt keyword triggers only on explicit phrases; plus numerous subagent-transcript, Remote Control, auth/credential, and`claude agents` fixes. v2..: preserves partial responses on mid-stream connection drops; fixes WSL2 mouse-wheel scrolling (v2.. regression), a sandbox`denyRead`/`allowRead` glob over a large tree making the Bash tool description enormous on Linux, the feedback-survey single-digit capture, welcome-screen promo stacking, Ctrl+O subagent transcript viewing, and remote background-task and plugin-loading reliability. v2..: CHANGELOG.md/feed.xml chore only. Current-session verification June 17, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases v2..–v2.. and the canonical changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and v2.. release notes, plus the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 17–19, 2026. v2..:`/config key=value` sets any setting from the prompt (e.g.`/config thinking=false`), in interactive,`-p`, and Remote Control;`sandbox.allowAppleEvents` opt-in setting lets sandboxed commands send Apple Events on macOS;`CLAUDE_CLIENT_PRESENCE_FILE` env var points at a marker file to suppress mobile push notifications while you’re at the machine; bundled Bun upgraded to 1.; long paragraphs stream line-by-line; mid-thinking API connection drops auto-retry; subagent panel idle-auto-hides after 30s and caps at 5 rows. v2..: auto mode blocks destructive git commands (`git reset --hard`,`git checkout -- .`,`git clean -fd`,`git stash drop`) when you didn’t ask to discard work,`git commit --amend` on commits not made by the agent this session, and`terraform destroy`/`pulumi destroy`/`cdk destroy` unless you asked for the specific stack; deprecated/auto-updated-model warnings shown on stderr in`-p` and for agent-frontmatter models;`attribution.sessionUrl` setting omits the claude.ai session link from commits and PRs;`/config --help` lists shorthand keys;`/config` UI Enter/Space toggle and Esc saves-and-closes; removed the startup “setup issues” line. (v2.. and v2.. were not released.) Current-session verification June 19, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases and the canonical changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and v2.. release notes, plus the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 20–22, 2026. v2..:`claude mcp login <name>` and`claude mcp logout <name>` authenticate/sign out of MCP servers from the CLI without opening`/mcp`, with`--no-browser` stdin redirect to complete over SSH;`!` bash commands now trigger Claude to respond to the output automatically (`"respondToBashCommands": false` keeps the prior context-only behavior);`Agent(type)` deny rules and`Agent(x,y)` allowed-types restrictions are now enforced for named subagent spawns; background subagents surface permission prompts in the main session (showing which agent asks; Esc denies just that tool) instead of auto-denying, and Esc/Ctrl+C stay responsive while background agents run after the main turn;`/review <pr>` uses the same review engine as`/code-review medium`;`/workflows` agent detail adds status filtering (press`f`);`/plugin` Installed adds a Skills section;`teammateMode: "iterm2"`(with a warning when auto mode can’t find the`it2` CLI);`/login` adds “Claude Platform on AWS - refresh credentials” when`awsAuthRefresh` is configured; skill frontmatter`display-name`/`default-enabled`/`fallback`/`metadata.*` accept kebab-case, snake_case, and camelCase, and malformed`SKILL.md` YAML loads the body with empty metadata instead of failing silently;`claude mcp get`/`claude mcp remove` suggest the closest configured server name on a typo; the agent is reminded to compact its`MEMORY.md` index near the size limit;`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES` caps at 15 (use`CLAUDE_CODE_RETRY_WATCHDOG` for unattended sessions). Fixes: streaming “Content block not found”/JSON parse errors after sleep-wake; subagent transcript scroll bleeding into the main transcript on exit; background task previews flashing raw tool names; Chrome tab-group isolation with the permissions gate off; duplicated background recaps;`Agent(type)` deny /`Agent(x,y)` allowed-types not enforced for named spawns;`~~strikethrough~~` rendering literal tildes; Workflow`agent({schema})` subagents looping forever on repeated schema-validation failures (now aborts after 5). v2..: the stream-stall hint now reads “Waiting for API response · will retry in …” (was “No response from API · Retrying in …”) and triggers after 20s of silence instead of 10s. v2.. was not released. Current-session verification June 23, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases v2..–v2.. and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, plus the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 23, 2026. v2..:`sandbox.credentials` setting blocks sandboxed commands from reading credential files and secret environment variables; org-configured model restrictions apply across the`/model` picker,`--model`,`/model`, and`ANTHROPIC_MODEL`, with a “restricted by your organization’s settings” message when a restricted model is selected; mouse-click selection support in fullscreen menus (permission prompts,`/model`,`/config`);`/install-github-app` makes the GitHub Actions workflow/secret setup optional;`/btw` adds ←/→ navigation through earlier answers;`/plugin` surfaces recently-unused plugins for cleanup;`claude --help` now lists the`--bg`/`--background` flag. Fixes:`--resume` failing with “No conversation found” when the original`-p` run produced no model turns;`--json-schema` and workflow`agent({schema})` structured output (no infinite`StructuredOutput` re-calls after success; follow-up turns reliably return structured output); remote MCP tool calls hanging for 5 minutes now abort with an error; Remote sessions ~2.7s slower to start after the agent-proxy CA trust install; pasted Korean/CJK mojibake in terminals delivering paste as per-byte extended-key events;`/update` over Remote Control hanging on a startup trust dialog; background jobs stuck in “working” when a turn ended without structured output; channel drops after navigating to the agents view /`/bg`/`/tui`/`/update`; agent stop-notification attribution and wording; subagent depth tracking on resume/fork; leaked agent worktree registrations cleaned up; Cmd+click URL open in Ghostty fullscreen on macOS; Esc/Ctrl-C/Ctrl-D during`/share` upload; [VSCode] extension unresponsive resuming a large session. Current-session verification June 23, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Anthropic, “Introducing Claude Tag,” anthropic.com, June 23, 2026: Introducing Claude Tag. Claude Tag is a Slack-native agent for Claude Enterprise and Team customers: members tag @Claude in a channel to delegate a task, which the agent decomposes into stages and completes asynchronously, posting results back to the channel; a shared single Claude identity lets teammates hand off in-progress work; administrators scope tool, data-source, and memory access per channel with token-spend limits and activity logs; it runs on Opus 4.. Anthropic reports its product team routes roughly 65% of its code changes through an internal version. Corroborated by contemporaneous coverage: Fortune and TechCrunch, June 23, 2026. Current-session verification June 24, 2026 (PST): the launch is corroborated across multiple independent outlets; specifics above reflect the Anthropic announcement and that coverage. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 24, 2026. v2..:`/rewind` supports resuming a conversation from before`/clear` was run; the sandbox network-permission dialog remembers allowed hosts for the session; ~37% CPU reduction during streaming responses; reduced long-session memory growth from the terminal-output cache. Fixes: stopping a background agent from the tasks panel is now permanent (no resurrection); scroll no longer jumps to the bottom while reading earlier output mid-stream;`/voice` explains an org-policy restriction instead of a generic “not available”;`/login` URL no longer truncates when wrapped in Windows Terminal; Cmd+click links in fullscreen Ghostty over ssh/tmux;`claude agents` no longer sends builtin slash commands (e.g.`/usage`) as prompt text and shows the`[Image #N]` placeholder for pasted images; hooks with comma-separated matchers (e.g.`Bash,PowerShell`) no longer silently never fire;`/permissions` Recently-denied approvals persist on close; the agent panel no longer jumps a row past the overflow cap; the welcome splash no longer overflows an 80×24 Terminal; managed`forceRemoteSettingsRefresh` now takes effect via MDM/file policy; improved MCP server reliability. Current-session verification June 25, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩

Claude Code Changelog (canonical), June 25–26, 2026 (v2.. and v2.. were not released). v2.. (June 25): new`autoMode.classifyAllShell` setting routes all Bash/PowerShell commands through the auto-mode classifier instead of only arbitrary-code-execution patterns; auto-mode denial reasons added to the transcript, the denial toast, and`/permissions` recent denials; new`claude_code.assistant_response` OpenTelemetry log event carries the model’s response text, redacted unless`OTEL_LOG_ASSISTANT_RESPONSES=1`— when that var is unset it follows`OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS`, so deployments already logging prompt content start receiving response content on upgrade (set`OTEL_LOG_ASSISTANT_RESPONSES=0` to keep prompts-only); live file-path autocomplete in bash mode (`!`); startup notice when MCP servers need authentication, pointing at`/mcp`; automatic memory-pressure reaping for idle background shell commands (disable with`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_BG_SHELL_PRESSURE_REAP=1`); plus fixes for stale`/model` state after`/login`, spurious backgrounding cancellation, phantom resumed-subagent spawns, and MCP`headersHelper` re-auth on 401/. v2.. (June 26): new`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_MOUSE_CLICKS` env var disables mouse click/drag/hover in fullscreen mode while keeping wheel scroll; hook matchers with hyphenated identifiers (e.g.`code-reviewer`,`mcp__brave-search`) now exact-match instead of substring-matching (use`mcp__brave-search__.*` to match all tools from a hyphenated MCP server); plus voice-dictation fixes (macOS device-change silence; auto-submit for space-less languages), plugin enable/consent fixes, and background-job/agent-daemon reliability fixes. Current-session verification June 30, 2026 (PST): the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirms v2.. as the latest release (v2.. was NOT present — a GitHub-summary claim of a “v2..” with org-default-models and clickable file attachments did not match the canonical changelog and was not used). ↩↩↩↩

Anthropic, canonical model documentation, Models overview and Introducing Claude Sonnet 5, June 30, 2026. Claude Sonnet 5 — model ID`claude-sonnet-5`(dateless pinned snapshot); Claude API alias`claude-sonnet-5`; AWS Bedrock`anthropic.claude-sonnet-5`; Google Cloud`claude-sonnet-5`. 1M-token context window; 128K max output (up to 300K on the Message Batches API with the`output-300k-2026-03-24` beta header); adaptive thinking (always on), no extended thinking; reliable knowledge cutoff January 2026. Pricing $3/MTok input and $15/MTok output, with introductory pricing of $2/$10 through August 31, 2026 (vs Opus 4. at $5/$25). Anthropic positions it as “the best combination of speed and intelligence,” with performance “close to that of Opus 4., but at lower prices,” a substantial improvement over Sonnet 4. on agentic reasoning/tool-use/coding/knowledge-work, a lower rate of undesirable behaviors, and much weaker cybersecurity capability than the Opus models. On Claude Code and the Claude API the`effort` parameter defaults to`high` on Sonnet 5. Current-session verification July 1, 2026 (PST): specs and pricing cross-checked against the canonical Anthropic model docs at platform.claude.com and the official announcement at anthropic.com/news/claude-sonnet-5. ↩↩

Claude Code Changelog (canonical), June 29 – July 2, 2026. v2.. (June 29): organization default models; readable default session names; clickable file attachments;`claude mcp list`/`get` no longer spawn repo-self-approved`.mcp.json` servers (untrusted workspaces show pending approval); streaming idle watchdog on by default with`CLAUDE_ENABLE_STREAM_WATCHDOG=0` opt-out;`/code-review` finders merged (~25% token cut); Remote Control disabled for non-Anthropic`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`; background-session reliability improvements. v2.. (June 30): Claude Sonnet 5 becomes the default model in Claude Code, with a native 1M-token context window and $2/$10 per MTok promotional pricing through August 31 (announcement). v2.. (July 1): subagents run in the background by default; Claude in Chrome generally available; background agents commit, push, and open a draft PR after worktree code work;`Notification` hook events`agent_needs_input`/`agent_completed`;`/dataviz` skill; Claude Platform on AWS as a gateway upstream; Explore agent inherits the session model (capped at Opus); subagents and compaction inherit extended-thinking configuration;`/agents` wizard removed. v2.. (July 2): stacked slash-skill invocations load all leading skills (up to 5); transient server 429s retry automatically for subscribers;`CLAUDE_CODE_RETRY_WATCHDOG` raises the non-capacity retry default to 300 and lifts the`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_RETRIES` cap of 15; extensive background-agent, subagent-error-reporting, and hook-stderr fixes. Verification note: the July-1 guide update recorded v2.. as not present in the canonical changelog at that day’s verification; the entry has since been published there, matching the early GitHub release summary. Current-session verification July 3, 2026 (PST): the canonical changelog confirms v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code Changelog (canonical) and GitHub releases, July 3–6, 2026. v2.. (July 3): the`default` permission mode is relabeled “Manual” across the CLI,`--help`, VS Code, and JetBrains, with`--permission-mode manual` and`"defaultMode": "manual"` accepted alongside the unchanged`default` config value;`AskUserQuestion` dialogs no longer auto-continue by default (opt into an idle timeout via`/config`); plus fixes for a startup crash on non-array`disabledMcpServers`/`enabledMcpServers`, background sessions stopping after sleep/wake, stale`daemon.lock` handling, subagents cut off by a rate limit before any output now returning cleanly, and tmux 3.+ synchronized-output flicker. v2.. (July 3): Claude Sonnet 5 sessions no longer use the mid-conversation system role for harness reminders. v2.. (July 6): a “Dynamic workflow size”`/config` setting (advisory small/medium/large agent counts, not an enforced cap);`workflow.run_id` and`workflow.name` OpenTelemetry attributes on workflow-spawned agents;`/review <pr>` reverts to a fast single-pass review (use`/code-review <level> <pr#>` for the multi-agent pass at a chosen effort); re-invoking an already-loaded skill no longer appends a duplicate copy of its instructions to context; a clearer MCP error suggesting`"type": "http"` when a server config has`url` but no`type`; plus Remote Control, resume-by-name performance in many-worktree repos, and installer/updater mid-download retry fixes. GitHub release timestamps: v2.. and v2.. published 2026-07-03, v2.. published 2026-07-06. Current-session verification July 7, 2026 (PST): the canonical changelog and GitHub releases confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Claude Code Changelog (canonical) and GitHub releases, July 7–9, 2026. v2.. (July 7): grey ⏸ footer badge when in manual permission mode; the session’s additional working directories are added to MCP`roots/list`, with`notifications/roots/list_changed` on changes; fixes include background sessions dropping a shell-exported`ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`(API keys were sent to the default endpoint and failed with 401), stale-daemon-token background sessions auto-recovering, worktree-isolated subagents sometimes executing in the parent checkout, and`TaskStop`/`TaskOutput` failing to find agents spawned by another agent. v2.. (July 8): bug-fix only — hook events not streaming during`SessionStart` hooks in headless sessions, which could idle-reap remote workers mid-hook. v2.. (July 9): auto mode blocks tampering with session transcript files; auto mode asks before running`rm -rf` on a variable it cannot resolve from context; background-task notifications explicitly state that no human input occurred, preventing fabricated in-transcript approvals from being acted on;`/doctor` becomes a full setup checkup that can diagnose and fix issues, with`/checkup` as an alias; “Claude Browser” reserved as an MCP server name (alongside “Claude Preview”); agent-view rows show a colored state word and classifier-written headline, and sessions that touch an existing PR link it in`claude agents`; auto-update downloads stream to disk (~400 MB lower peak memory). Current-session verification July 9, 2026 (PST): the canonical changelog confirms v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. July 2026. v2..:`/login` supports authenticating through Anthropic-operated public gateway endpoints;`/commit-push-pr` auto-allows`git push` to the repository’s configured push remote —`remote.pushDefault` if set, or the sole remote — rather than only`origin`. Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. July 2026. v2..: Auto Mode no longer requires the`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE` opt-in on AWS Bedrock, Google Vertex AI, and Microsoft Foundry — administrators disable it with the`disableAutoMode` managed setting; Bedrock, Vertex AI, and Claude Platform on AWS now default to Claude Opus 4.; the`autoMode` setting is no longer read from a repository’s`.claude/settings.local.json`(use`~/.claude/settings.json`); plugin security —`${user_config.*}` interpolation is rejected in shell-form plugin commands (use the exec form or the`$CLAUDE_PLUGIN_OPTION_<KEY>` env var), and`pluginConfigs` is no longer read from project-level`.claude/settings.json`. Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog. ↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, the Claude Code accessibility docs, and the Claude Code corporate launcher docs. July 2026. v2..: screen reader mode via`claude --ax-screen-reader`,`CLAUDE_AX_SCREEN_READER=1`, or the`"axScreenReader": true` setting, documented on the new accessibility page; the`processWrapper` setting /`CLAUDE_CODE_PROCESS_WRAPPER` env var routes all Claude Code self-spawned processes through a wrapper executable for corporate launcher integration; the`vimInsertModeRemaps` setting defines vim insert-mode key remaps (e.g.`jj`→ Escape); catastrophic removals (such as`rm -rf ~`) inside command substitution —`$(...)`, backticks,`<(...)`— now prompt even in`--dangerously-skip-permissions` and auto mode. Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the code.claude.com docs pages. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. July 2026. v2..: Claude Code now shows a startup warning for permission rules using the`Write(path)`,`NotebookEdit(path)`, or`Glob(path)` forms — write-side file rules should use`Edit(path)`(which governs all file-writing tools) and read-side rules`Read(path)`(which governs file-reading tools, Glob included). Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog. ↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. July 2026. v2..: the`--forward-subagent-text` flag and`CLAUDE_CODE_FORWARD_SUBAGENT_TEXT` env var include subagent text and thinking output in`--output-format stream-json`; “Always allow” permission rules are now saved at the repository root, so approvals granted in one worktree persist across sessions and across all worktrees of the repository. Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes. July 2026. v2..:`/fork` now copies the conversation into a new background session with its own row in`claude agents`, and the previous in-session fork behavior is renamed`/subtask`; new`claude auto-mode reset` subcommand with`--yes` to skip confirmation; per-session budgets — WebSearch calls and subagent spawns each default to 200 per session via`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_WEB_SEARCHES_PER_SESSION` and`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENTS_PER_SESSION`, with`/clear` resetting the subagent budget; MCP tool calls running longer than 2 minutes automatically move to the background, threshold configurable via`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_AUTO_BACKGROUND_MS`; the Task tool’s`mode` parameter is deprecated and ignored — subagents inherit the parent session’s permission mode; the enterprise`forceLoginMethod` setting is now enforced for the VS Code extension, the SDK,`claude setup-token`, and`/install-github-app`(previously terminal-only). Verified for this guide update on July 17, 2026 against the GitHub release and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. entry. July 2026. v2..: single-segment`dir/**` allow rules (e.g.`Edit(src/**)`) now match only`<cwd>/dir` instead of any nested`dir/` in the tree —`deny`/`ask` rules keep any-depth matching, and hook`if:` conditions gain the same cwd scoping (write`**/dir/**` for any-depth); permission fail-closed hardening — Windows PowerShell 5. bypass fixed, Bash file-descriptor redirect forms fail closed, commands over 10,000 characters always prompt, zsh subscripts/modifiers in`[[ ]]` prompt, unsafe`help`/`man` variants no longer auto-approved, remote-session prompts cannot proceed before local confirmation,`docker`/Podman daemon-redirect flags (`--url`,`--connection`,`--identity`, remote mode) prompt, and`file -m`/`--magic-file`/`-f`/`--files-from` requires permission; new EndConversation tool for ending sessions on highly abusive input or jailbreak attempts; SessionStart hooks report source`"fork"` for forked sessions (previously`"resume"`); OTel log events gain`message.uuid`,`client_request_id`, and`tool_source` attributes,`CLAUDE_CODE_OTEL_CONTENT_MAX_LENGTH` configures the 60 KB content-truncation limit, and the`subagentStatusLine` payload includes reasoning effort. Verified for this guide update on July 21, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog. (v2.. was never released.) ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. entry. July 2026. v2.. (single change): Claude no longer runs the`/verify` and`/code-review` skills on its own — invoke them with`/verify` or`/code-review` when you want them. Verified for this guide update on July 21, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog. ↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. entry. July 2026. v2..: adds the`sandbox.filesystem.disabled` setting to skip filesystem isolation while keeping network egress control; fixes a quadratic message-normalization slowdown in long sessions, auto-mode HTTP 401 denials after OAuth token rotation, worktree-isolated subagents redirecting git into the shared checkout via`git -C`/`--git-dir`/`GIT_DIR`, and`.claude` symlink write redirection;`/rewind` refuses symlink/hardlink restore paths;`/context` warns on context-window overflow; mid-session skill/command edits appear in the slash menu without restart; the`/fork` confirmation shows the new session’s name and`claude attach` id; cloud sessions recover the in-flight turn after a mid-turn container restart. Verified for this guide update on July 21, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog. ↩↩↩

Anthropic research: Claude’s ability to end a rare subset of conversations. 2025. Background on the conversation-ending capability that v2.. brings to Claude Code as the EndConversation tool — deployed on claude.ai since 2025 for rare cases of persistently abusive or harmful interactions. ↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. entry. July 2026. v2..: subagents no longer spawn nested subagents by default — set`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH` to allow deeper nesting (reversing the v2.. recursive-spawning default); new cap on concurrently-running subagents, default 20, override with`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS`;`--max-budget-usd` now stops background subagents — once the cap is reached, new spawns are denied and running background agents are halted; emoji shortcode autocomplete in the prompt input (`:heart:`→ ❤️), disabled via the`emojiCompletionEnabled` setting. Plus reliability and isolation fixes: symlinked working directories canonicalized for background-session isolation, managed`OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` governing all signals, transcript-write-failure warnings, MCP truncated-output memory leak, Windows auto-update`claude.exe` restore, Bedrock Opus 4. auto-compact, Claude Desktop corporate mTLS/TLS/OAuth/proxy settings,`--resume` malformed-attachment TypeError, unstoppable background shells, brace-expansion OOM, Remote Control late-joiner prompts, screen-reader fixes, ssh/tmux-clickable footer PR badges (`FORCE_HYPERLINK=0` opts out), the login-expiry warning moving from 5 to 3 days, and the frontend-design plugin tip capped at 3 lifetime impressions. Verified for this guide update on July 22, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog. ↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. entry. July 2026. v2..:`/code-review` runs as a background subagent so review work no longer fills the conversation, with stacked slash commands remaining its review target; skills with`context: fork` run in the background by default, with per-skill opt-out via the new`background: false` frontmatter key; the dangerous-`rm`, background-`&`, and suspicious-Windows-path checks no longer open permission dialogs — the auto-mode classifier adjudicates them — and plan mode with auto no longer prompts for Bash commands the static analyzer can’t prove read-only; agent frontmatter hooks require the agent file’s own folder to have accepted workspace trust before running;`/deep-research` starts only when invoked manually. Plus: left-arrow discard confirmation and Esc-returns-from-agent-view; benign server-managed feature/cost toggles skip the settings-approval prompt; agent names with`:` rejected;`yes`/`no`/`on`/`off`/`1`/`0` accepted for skill and plugin frontmatter booleans; trust dialogs name the repository root; improved sandbox restrictions for IDE interactions;`Ctrl+B` background-shell caps; Bedrock ARN spend metering; fork-session lineage after compaction in headless/SDK sessions; Windows`\u`-path corruption fix; remote-session zombie-heartbeat fix. Verified for this guide update on July 24, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog: v2.. published 2026-07-22T21:24Z is npm latest. (Attribution correction, July 25, 2026: the`claude mcp list`/`/mcp` HTTP-status-and-error-text item and the MCP hidden-whitespace warning were originally listed here under v2..; the raw changelog places both in the v2.. entry. Moved to 196.) ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG, v2.. and v2.. entries. July 2026. v2.. (July 24): adds Claude Opus 5 (`claude-opus-5`) as the new default Opus model — 1M context, fast mode at $10/$50 per MTok; adds the`sandbox.network.strictAllowlist` setting to deny non-allowlisted hosts for sandboxed commands without prompting; adds the`DirectoryAdded` hook, which fires after`/add-dir` or the SDK`register_repo_root` control request registers a new working directory mid-session; adds`mcp_server_errors` to the headless stream-json init event, listing`--mcp-config` entries skipped by config validation (terminal runs print a startup warning); adds the`workflowSizeGuideline` settings key so the advisory Dynamic workflow size guideline can be set from any settings file, with the`/config` row hidden while one does; adds nested subagent forwarding in stream-json, so subagents spawned at depth-2+ appear when`--forward-subagent-text` is set, keyed by their spawning Agent`tool_use` id; changes dynamic workflows to default to a medium size guideline (aim for fewer than 15 agents); removes Opus 4. from fast mode —`/fast` now applies to Opus 5 and Opus 4.; subagents can now spawn nested subagents up to depth 3 by default (was 1); set`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH=1` to disable nesting; updates the`claude-api` skill to default to Claude Opus 5 with a migration path from Opus 4.; adds HTTP status and error text to`claude mcp list` and`/mcp` when a server fails to connect, plus a warning for MCP config values with hidden leading or trailing whitespace; changes managed MCP allowlist/denylist`${VAR}` entries to resolve from the startup environment and managed-settings env instead of settings-file env; adds the current default workflow size to the running-workflow status line; fixes`claude -p` text output dropping the answer already produced when a turn dies on a mid-stream API error, the Fable row showing a stale “Requires usage credits” label, the`/model` picker showing the merged Opus row as plain “Opus” instead of “Opus (1M context)”, the new-release highlight covering more than the newest model’s name, GNU screen copy-on-select printing base, Remote Control clients keeping a stale fast-mode status after a model switch/reconnect/failed org check,`CLAUDE_CODE_GIT_BASH_PATH` on Windows exiting or being used as bash when the path isn’t a bash/sh binary (now ignored with a warning), Vim mode ← on an empty prompt not returning to the agent view from NORMAL mode, and screen-reader mode rewriting the entire input line on every keystroke; improves the “Remote Control is only available via api.anthropic.com” error to name the setting that caused it and`claude --teleport` to show which repo the current checkout points at on a mismatch. v2.. (July 25): bug fixes and reliability improvements only — no user-facing feature, setting, command, or pricing changes. Verified for this guide update on July 25, 2026 against the raw GitHub changelog. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Anthropic, Introducing Claude Opus 5, plus canonical model documentation: Models overview and Migrating from Claude Opus 4. to Claude Opus 5. July 2026. Claude Opus 5: model ID`claude-opus-5`(dateless pinned snapshot; Bedrock`anthropic.claude-opus-5`, Google Cloud`claude-opus-5`); “For complex agentic coding and enterprise work”; 1M token context window, 128K max output (300K on the Message Batches API with the`output-300k-2026-03-24` beta header); pricing $5 / input MTok, $25 / output MTok — “the same as Opus 4.”; adaptive thinking Yes,`thinking.type: "enabled"` extended thinking No; reliable knowledge cutoff May 2026, training data cutoff May 2026; the`effort` parameter “defaults to`high` on the Claude API and Claude Code,” levels`low`,`medium`,`high`,`xhigh`,`max`. Anthropic states it is “the new default model on Claude Max, and the strongest model on Claude Pro,” delivers “greatly improved performance for the same cost,” and “generates 26% fewer tokens on average” at max reasoning versus Opus 4.. Breaking changes from the migration guide: (1) “On Claude Opus 4., requests without a`thinking` field run without thinking; on Claude Opus 5, the same requests run with adaptive thinking”; (2) “You can still turn thinking off with`thinking: {type: \"disabled\"}`, but only at an effort level of`high` or below. A request that combines`thinking: {type: \"disabled\"}` with effort`xhigh` or`max` returns a 400 error” — “enforced on each request.” Claude Opus 4. now appears in the Models overview Legacy models table. Effort-level availability from Effort:`xhigh` is “Available on Claude Fable 5, Claude Mythos 5, Claude Opus 5, Claude Opus 4., Claude Opus 4., and Claude Sonnet 5”;`max` adds Claude Opus 4. and Claude Sonnet 4.; “Claude Opus 5 supports all five effort levels” and “The API default is`high`.” Fetched and verified July 25, 2026. ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Fast mode (research preview), Anthropic platform documentation. Verified July 25, 2026. “Fast mode delivers up to 2.5x higher output tokens per second from Claude Opus 5 and Claude Opus 4. at premium pricing. Set`speed: \"fast\"` with the`fast-mode-2026-02-01` beta header on your request to opt in.” Supported models: Claude Opus 5 (`claude-opus-5`) and Claude Opus 4. (`claude-opus-4-8`) — $10 USD / MTok input, $50 USD / MTok output, “priced at a multiplier on standard rates across the full context window, including requests over 200k input tokens.” Research preview on the Claude API (including Claude Managed Agents) only: “It is not available on Amazon Bedrock, Google Cloud, or Microsoft Foundry,” nor on Claude Platform on AWS, the Batch API, or with a Priority Tier commitment. On Opus 4.: “Fast mode is not available on Claude Opus 4.. Requests to`claude-opus-4-7` with`speed: \"fast\"` return an error; unlike Claude Opus 4. …, requests do not fall back to standard speed.” On Opus 4.: requests “do not return an error: they run at standard speed and are billed at standard rates … and the response reports`usage.speed: \"standard\"`.” Same weights and behavior as standard speed; gains are in output tokens per second, not time to first token. Dedicated rate limit with`anthropic-fast-*-tokens-*` headers; “Switching between fast and standard speed invalidates the prompt cache.” ↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 24, 2026. v2..: bug fixes and reliability improvements only — no user-facing feature, setting, command, or pricing changes. (v2.. and v2.. were not released.) Current-session verification June 24, 2026 (PST): GitHub releases and the canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, the Claude Code Changelog (canonical), and Anthropic news: Claude Fable 5. June 9, 2026. v2.. makes Claude Fable 5 selectable in Claude Code (`/model claude-fable-5` after`claude update`) — a new tier above Opus, model ID`claude-fable-5`, 1M context / 128K output, $10/$50 per MTok, with safety classifiers that fall back to Opus 4. on cyber/bio-chem/distillation queries and a rollout to subscription plans through June 22, 2026. Fable 5 shares Opus 4.’s request surface (adaptive thinking only;`temperature`/`top_p`/`top_k` and`budget_tokens` removed) but adds one breaking change — an explicit`thinking: {type: "disabled"}` returns HTTP 400, so omit the parameter to run without thinking. The release also fixes sessions launched from the VS Code integrated terminal (or any shell inheriting Claude Code environment variables) failing to save transcripts and not appearing under`--resume`. Pricing, context window, and request-surface details cross-checked against the bundled`claude-api` model catalog (cached 2026-05-26). Claude Code-specific Fable 5 behavior — the`fable`/`best` aliases, the full`low`–`max` effort scale (`high` default), thinking that cannot be disabled (session toggle /`alwaysThinkingEnabled`/`MAX_THINKING_TOKENS=0` all no-ops), the`claude-fable-5[1m]` extended-context variant, and the`ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_FABLE_MODEL`/`DISABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_FABLE` config — verified against the Claude Code model configuration docs on June 10, 2026. Current-session verification June 9–10, 2026 (PST): GitHub release`v2..` published June 9 (17:23Z) confirmed as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 8, 2026. Adds the`--safe-mode` flag and`CLAUDE_CODE_SAFE_MODE` env var (launch with CLAUDE.md, plugins, skills, hooks, and MCP servers all disabled for troubleshooting), the`/cd` command (move a session to a new working directory without breaking the prompt cache), and the`disableBundledSkills` setting plus`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_BUNDLED_SKILLS` env var (hide bundled skills, workflows, and built-in slash commands from the model). Plus ~20 fixes: wrapped-row Up/Down navigation, enterprise MCP policy enforcement on reconnect and IDE-typed configs, a 30–50ms macOS UI stall with claude.ai credentials,`claude -p` Windows performance, Remote Control reconnection, Windows Git Credential Manager popup, custom-statusline footer hints, stale remote-session prompts,`claude agents --json` omitting blocked sessions, TaskCreate input auto-repair, lower streaming CPU, restored Vertex/Foundry idle timeout, skill-tag contrast, and background-session flag preservation across retire→wake. Current-session verification June 9, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog and GitHub releases confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 6, 2026. v2.. adds the`fallbackModel` setting (up to three fallback models tried in order when the primary is overloaded/unavailable), extends`--fallback-model` to interactive sessions, and retries a turn once on the fallback model for unexpected non-retryable API errors; adds glob support in the deny-rule tool-name position (`"*"` denies all tools) while allow rules reject non-MCP globs; hardens cross-session messaging so`SendMessage`-relayed messages from other Claude sessions no longer carry user authority; makes`MAX_THINKING_TOKENS=0`,`--thinking disabled`, and the per-model thinking toggle disable thinking on think-by-default models; and makes`claude update` announce the target version before downloading, plus fixes for image processing, remote-session stability, and terminal rendering (JetBrains, WezTerm/Ghostty). v2.. and v2.. (both June 6) are bug-fix/reliability-only with no individually documented user-facing changes. Current-session verification June 6, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog and GitHub releases confirm v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 4, 2026; the changelog jumps from v2.. to v2.. with no published 161/. Adds`requiredMinimumVersion`/`requiredMaximumVersion` managed settings (refuse to start outside an approved version range),`/plugin list` with`--enabled`/`--disabled` filters, Stop/SubagentStop`hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` for non-error feedback that keeps the turn going,`/btw`“c to copy” raw markdown, skills`\$` literal-dollar escape, and stdio MCP servers receiving the session’s`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` on`--resume`. Fixes include`claude -p` hanging on never-exiting background commands, Bedrock/Vertex/Foundry`claude -p` under`CI=true` demanding an Anthropic key, the v2..`$TMPDIR` regression breaking bazel/EDR-protected Go, OneDrive/read-only session-env`EEXIST` on Windows, late-applying org-managed permission rules, background sessions losing tasks across updates, hook`if: "Bash(...)"` over-matching`$()`/`$VAR`, and`~`-path deny rules not blocking`$HOME` references. Current-session verification June 4, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog confirms v2.. as latest. ↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes and the Claude Code Changelog (canonical). June 2, 2026. Adds permission prompts before writing shell startup files (`.zshenv`,`.zlogin`,`.bash_login`) and`~/.config/git/`;`acceptEdits` now prompts before build-tool config files that grant code execution (`.npmrc`,`.yarnrc*`,`bunfig.toml`,`.bazelrc`,`.pre-commit-config.yaml`,`.devcontainer/`, and similar). A single-file`grep`/`egrep`/`fgrep` now satisfies the read-before-edit check. Renames the dynamic-workflow trigger keyword from`workflow` to`ultracode`(violet-highlighted in the prompt input); the word “workflow” no longer triggers a run. Removes`CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE`(now a no-op) and the JetBrains plugin install suggestion at startup. Plus ~25 fixes: WSL copy-on-select via PowerShell interop instead of OSC 52,`claude agents` session restore dropping chat history and re-running the original prompt, overnight-retired background sessions losing conversation,`claude --bg`“socket missing” cold-start race, Windows background-session input unresponsiveness under heavy CPU, terminal sync-output artifacts on Apple Terminal/tmux, CJK IME caret position in the`claude agents` view,`file:///C:/...` link rewriting on Windows hyperlink terminals, voice mode failing on non-ASCII project/branch names, the auto-mode-unavailable message on third-party providers now pointing to`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE` instead of blaming the model,`/effort ultracode` no longer offered on models that cannot run xhigh, model-not-found errors no longer suggesting`--model` under the SDK, vim`p` paste position after`v$` yank, and SIGTERM-before-SIGKILL on background-session teardown. Current-session verification on June 2, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirmed v2.. as the latest release. ↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, and Claude Code Changelog (canonical). v2.. and v2.. (May 29, 2026) ship the same Opus 4. thinking-block mutation fix that surfaced as API errors. v2.. (May 29, 2026) makes`.claude/skills/` plugins auto-load without marketplace registration, adds`claude plugin init <name>` scaffolding, gives`/plugin` argument autocomplete, lets`EnterWorktree` switch between Claude-managed worktrees mid-session, leaves background worktrees unlocked after the agent finishes so`git worktree remove`/`prune` work without manual unlocks, includes`tool_parameters` in`tool_decision` telemetry events when`OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS=1`, plus fixes for unprocessable-image crashes (now text placeholders), sandbox network permission prompts in auto/bypass mode across desktop / IDE / SDK surfaces, background-session retire-on-park, agents-list Esc cancellation of “opening…”, background worktrees orphaned by the 30-day retention sweep,`--resume` background-subagent reporting, terminal rendering / clipboard in tmux + VS Code + Cursor + Windsurf, WSL image / screenshot / drag paths, long-resumed-conversation performance, and`/terminal-setup` disabling GPU acceleration in integrated terminals. v2.. (May 30, 2026) makes Auto Mode available on Bedrock, Vertex, and Foundry for Opus 4. and Opus 4. with`CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_AUTO_MODE=1`. v2.. (May 31, 2026) is internal infrastructure only with no announced user-facing changes. Current-session verification on May 31, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirmed v2..–v2.. entries; v2.. confirmed as the latest release. ↩↩↩↩

Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, Claude Code v2.. release notes, and Claude Code Changelog (canonical). v2.. (May 23, 2026) is internal infrastructure only. v2.. (May 27, 2026) introduces`/code-review --fix` apply-to-working-tree,`disallowed-tools` skill/command frontmatter,`/reload-skills`,`SessionStart` hook`reloadSkills`/`sessionTitle` outputs, the`MessageDisplay` hook event,`pluginSuggestionMarketplaces` managed setting,`--fallback-model` mid-session switching, removal of auto-mode opt-in, vim`/` reverse history, plus rendering, performance, and reliability fixes. v2.. (May 28, 2026) adds`skipLfs` to plugin marketplaces, makes`/model` save as the new-session default (with`s` for session-only and a`modelPicker:thisSessionOnly` keybind replacing`modelPicker:setAsDefault`), and puts`COLUMNS`/`LINES` in status-line env; macOS background agents persist Privacy & Security grants; plus 25+ fixes covering stateful MCP`tools/list` reconnect loops, custom-gateway OAuth-credential leak, subagent MCP policy enforcement, Windows installer / update channel fixes, and background-session attach reliability. v2.. (May 28, 2026) makes Opus 4. the default with high effort by default and a new`/effort xhigh` level, introduces dynamic workflows orchestrating tens to hundreds of agents in the background via`/workflows`, makes Fast mode on Opus 4. available at 2× standard rate for 2.× speed, defaults the lean system prompt for all models except Haiku/Sonnet/Opus 4.-and-earlier, narrows the multiple-choice-question prompt to genuinely unmakeable decisions, reverts`/simplify` to cleanup-only review separate from`/code-review --fix`’s bug-hunting pass, relabels`/effort` slider to “Faster”/”Smarter”, adds`claude agents``! <command>` background-shell sessions plus`claude --bg --exec '<command>'`, lets plugins declare`defaultEnabled: false`, pins suggested plugins to the current directory in`/plugin` Discover, makes streaming tool execution always-on, passes`CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID` and`CLAUDECODE=1` to stdio MCP subprocess env, marks unapproved`.mcp.json` servers as`⏸ Pending approval` instead of auto-connecting, and deprecates`CLAUDE_CODE_OPUS_4_6_FAST_MODE_OVERRIDE`(removed June 1; switch to`/model claude-opus-4-6[1m]` then`/fast on`). Current-session verification on May 28, 2026 (PST): canonical changelog at code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog confirmed v2..–v2.. entries; v2.. confirmed as the latest release. ↩↩↩

Claude Code CHANGELOG— composite reference for 23 env vars documented in April 2026 parity audit. OTel exporters (`OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER`,`OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER`,`OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER`) gained`none`-disable handling in v2... Sensitive-field gating:`OTEL_LOG_TOOL_DETAILS`(v2..`tool_parameters`),`OTEL_LOG_TOOL_CONTENT` and`OTEL_LOG_USER_PROMPTS`(v2.. opt-in, sensitive by default).`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC` disables release-notes fetch (v2..); v2.. also stops the auto-title Haiku request in headless/SDK when set. API/model:`CLAUDE_CODE_EXTRA_BODY`(v2.. fix: no more 400 on Vertex/subagent with`output_config.effort`),`CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_CONTEXT_TOKENS`(pre-existing; v2.. fixed`DISABLE_COMPACT` interaction),`CLAUDE_CODE_FILE_READ_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS`(v2..),`CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONSTREAMING_FALLBACK`(v2..),`ANTHROPIC_BETAS`(v2.. fix on Haiku),`ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL`(v0.. stopped escaping Bedrock ARN slashes). Plugins/MCP:`CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_CACHE_DIR`(v2.. fixed literal-tilde dir),`CLAUDE_CODE_PLUGIN_KEEP_MARKETPLACE_ON_FAILURE`(v2.. offline-friendly),`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_SERVER_NAME`+`CLAUDE_CODE_MCP_SERVER_URL`(v2..`headersHelper` context). Shell/IDE:`CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL_PREFIX`(v1..),`CLAUDE_CODE_GIT_BASH_PATH`(v2.. Windows),`CLAUDE_CODE_EXIT_AFTER_STOP_DELAY`(v2.. SDK idle),`CLAUDE_CODE_AUTO_CONNECT_IDE`(v1..). Enterprise:`CLAUDE_CODE_PROXY_RESOLVES_HOSTS`(v2.. moved from default-on to opt-in),`CLAUDE_CODE_API_KEY_HELPER_TTL_MS`(apiKeyHelper refresh + 5-min default added v0..; env var for overriding the TTL added v0..). Also:`ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H_BEDROCK`— Bedrock-scoped alias for`ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H`; deprecated in v2.. but still honored with a deprecation notice. ↩↩↩↩↩
